books/models.py

from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User


class Category(models.Model):
    """图书分类"""
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "图书分类"
        verbose_name_plural = "图书分类"
        ordering = ["name"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name


class Book(models.Model):
    """图书"""
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        AVAILABLE = "available", "可借阅"
        BORROWED = "borrowed", "已借出"
        MAINTENANCE = "maintenance", "维护中"

    title = models.CharField(max_length=300, verbose_name="书名")
    author = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name="作者")
    isbn = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True, verbose_name="ISBN")
    category = models.ForeignKey(
        Category, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True,
        related_name="books", verbose_name="分类"
    )
    publisher = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name="出版社")
    publish_date = models.DateField(verbose_name="出版日期")
    price = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, verbose_name="定价")
    total_copies = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1, verbose_name="总册数")
    available_copies = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1, verbose_name="可借册数")
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.AVAILABLE,
        verbose_name="状态"
    )
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name="简介")
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name="添加时间")
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True, verbose_name="更新时间")

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "图书"
        verbose_name_plural = "图书"
        ordering = ["-created_at"]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["title"]),
            models.Index(fields=["author"]),
            models.Index(fields=["isbn"]),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.title} - {self.author}"

    def update_status(self):
        """自动更新状态"""
        if self.available_copies == 0:
            self.status = self.Status.BORROWED
        else:
            self.status = self.Status.AVAILABLE
        self.save(update_fields=["status"])


class BorrowRecord(models.Model):
    """借阅记录"""
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        BORROWED = "borrowed", "借阅中"
        RETURNED = "returned", "已归还"
        OVERDUE = "overdue", "逾期"

    book = models.ForeignKey(
        Book, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="borrow_records",
        verbose_name="图书"
    )
    borrower = models.ForeignKey(
        User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="borrow_records",
        verbose_name="借阅人"
    )
    borrow_date = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, verbose_name="借阅日期")
    due_date = models.DateTimeField(verbose_name="应还日期")
    return_date = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name="实际归还日期")
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.BORROWED,
        verbose_name="状态"
    )

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "借阅记录"
        verbose_name_plural = "借阅记录"
        ordering = ["-borrow_date"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.borrower.username} 借阅 {self.book.title}"

books/admin.py

from django.contrib import admin
from django.utils.html import format_html
from .models import Category, Book, BorrowRecord


@admin.register(Category)
class CategoryAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("name", "description", "created_at", "book_count")
    search_fields = ("name",)

    def book_count(self, obj):
        return obj.books.count()
    book_count.short_description = "图书数量"


@admin.register(Book)
class BookAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "title", "author", "isbn", "category", "price",
        "available_copies", "status_colored", "created_at"
    )
    list_filter = ("status", "category", "publish_date")
    search_fields = ("title", "author", "isbn")
    ordering = ("-created_at",)
    list_per_page = 30
    readonly_fields = ("created_at", "updated_at")
    fieldsets = (
        ("基本信息", {"fields": ("title", "author", "isbn", "description")}),
        ("出版信息", {"fields": ("publisher", "publish_date", "price")}),
        ("库存信息", {"fields": ("category", "total_copies", "available_copies", "status")}),
        ("时间信息", {"fields": ("created_at", "updated_at")}),
    )

    def status_colored(self, obj):
        """带颜色的状态显示"""
        colors = {
            "available": "green",
            "borrowed": "red",
            "maintenance": "orange",
        }
        color = colors.get(obj.status, "black")
        return format_html(
            '<span style="color: {}; font-weight: bold;">{}</span>',
            color, obj.get_status_display()
        )
    status_colored.short_description = "状态"


@admin.register(BorrowRecord)
class BorrowRecordAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("id", "book", "borrower", "borrow_date", "due_date", "status")
    list_filter = ("status", "borrow_date")
    search_fields = ("book__title", "borrower__username")
    date_hierarchy = "borrow_date"
    readonly_fields = ("borrow_date",)

books/serializers.py

from rest_framework import serializers
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.utils import timezone
from datetime import timedelta
from .models import Category, Book, BorrowRecord


class CategorySerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    book_count = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = Category
        fields = ["id", "name", "description", "book_count", "created_at"]

    def get_book_count(self, obj):
        return obj.books.count()


class BookListSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    """列表用（减少字段，提高性能）"""
    category_name = serializers.CharField(source="category.name", read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = Book
        fields = [
            "id", "title", "author", "isbn", "category_name",
            "price", "status", "available_copies", "publish_date"
        ]


class BookDetailSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    """详情用（包含全部字段）"""
    category = CategorySerializer(read_only=True)
    category_id = serializers.PrimaryKeyRelatedField(
        queryset=Category.objects.all(), source="category", write_only=True
    )
    borrow_count = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = Book
        fields = "__all__"

    def get_borrow_count(self, obj):
        return obj.borrow_records.count()

    def validate_isbn(self, value):
        """自定义 ISBN 校验"""
        if len(value) not in [10, 13]:
            raise serializers.ValidationError("ISBN 必须是 10 位或 13 位")
        return value


class BorrowRecordSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    book_title = serializers.CharField(source="book.title", read_only=True)
    borrower_name = serializers.CharField(source="borrower.username", read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = BorrowRecord
        fields = [
            "id", "book", "book_title", "borrower_name",
            "borrow_date", "due_date", "return_date", "status"
        ]
        read_only_fields = ["borrow_date", "status"]


class BorrowCreateSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    """借阅请求——不需要传入所有字段"""
    book_id = serializers.IntegerField()

    def validate_book_id(self, value):
        try:
            book = Book.objects.get(id=value)
        except Book.DoesNotExist:
            raise serializers.ValidationError("图书不存在")

        if book.available_copies <= 0:
            raise serializers.ValidationError("该图书已全部借出")
        return value


class ReturnSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    """归还请求"""
    record_id = serializers.IntegerField()

books/views.py

from rest_framework import viewsets, status
from rest_framework.decorators import action
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated, AllowAny
from django.utils import timezone
from datetime import timedelta
from .models import Category, Book, BorrowRecord
from .serializers import (
    CategorySerializer, BookListSerializer, BookDetailSerializer,
    BorrowRecordSerializer, BorrowCreateSerializer, ReturnSerializer
)


class CategoryViewSet(viewsets.ReadOnlyModelViewSet):
    """分类——只读"""
    queryset = Category.objects.all()
    serializer_class = CategorySerializer
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]


class BookViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """图书——增删改查"""
    queryset = Book.objects.select_related("category").all()
    filterset_fields = ["status", "category"]
    search_fields = ["title", "author", "isbn"]
    ordering_fields = ["title", "price", "publish_date", "created_at"]

    def get_serializer_class(self):
        if self.action == "list":
            return BookListSerializer
        return BookDetailSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ["create", "update", "partial_update", "destroy"]:
            return [IsAuthenticated()]
        return [AllowAny()]

    @action(detail=True, methods=["post"])
    def borrow(self, request, pk=None):
        """借阅图书"""
        book = self.get_object()
        serializer = BorrowCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        if book.available_copies <= 0:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "该图书已全部借出"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        # 创建借阅记录
        record = BorrowRecord.objects.create(
            book=book,
            borrower=request.user,
            due_date=timezone.now() + timedelta(days=30),
        )

        # 更新库存
        book.available_copies -= 1
        book.update_status()

        return Response(
            BorrowRecordSerializer(record).data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
        )

    @action(detail=True, methods=["post"])
    def return_book(self, request, pk=None):
        """归还图书"""
        book = self.get_object()
        record_id = request.data.get("record_id")

        try:
            record = BorrowRecord.objects.get(
                id=record_id, book=book, status=BorrowRecord.Status.BORROWED
            )
        except BorrowRecord.DoesNotExist:
            return Response(
                {"detail": "借阅记录不存在或已归还"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        record.return_date = timezone.now()
        record.status = BorrowRecord.Status.RETURNED
        record.save()

        book.available_copies += 1
        book.update_status()

        return Response(BorrowRecordSerializer(record).data)


class BorrowRecordViewSet(viewsets.ReadOnlyModelViewSet):
    """借阅记录——只读"""
    serializer_class = BorrowRecordSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def get_queryset(self):
        """普通用户只看自己的，管理员看全部"""
        queryset = BorrowRecord.objects.select_related("book", "borrower").all()
        if not self.request.user.is_staff:
            queryset = queryset.filter(borrower=self.request.user)
        return queryset

    @action(detail=False, methods=["get"])
    def my_borrows(self, request):
        """我的当前借阅"""
        records = BorrowRecord.objects.filter(
            borrower=request.user,
            status=BorrowRecord.Status.BORROWED
        )
        serializer = self.get_serializer(records, many=True)
        return Response(serializer.data)

books/urls.py

from rest_framework.routers import DefaultRouter
from .views import CategoryViewSet, BookViewSet, BorrowRecordViewSet

router = DefaultRouter()
router.register(r"categories", CategoryViewSet, basename="category")
router.register(r"books", BookViewSet, basename="book")
router.register(r"borrows", BorrowRecordViewSet, basename="borrow")

urlpatterns = router.urls

urlpatterns = [
    path("admin/", admin.site.urls),
    path("api/", include("books.urls")),
